### [1] [Assessing the Downstream Utility of Evidence-Aware Retrieval in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26379)
*Utshab Kumar Ghosh,Debayan Mukhopadhyay,Shubham Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了检索评估信号（答案支持信号）与RAG下游任务对齐的价值。通过五个检索基准和TREC RAG 2025端到端实验，发现该信号虽然改变了检索排名，但其在下游任务中的价值并不一致：不能可靠改善检索器训练、对系统选择的影响取决于生成器指令、无法稳健预测未知主题的答案质量，且在证据过滤中对答案改进的结论受评估者影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG的检索评估正从主题相关性转向是否包含可支持生成的证据，但鲜有研究验证这种与下游证据需求更紧密的对齐是否真正提升了基于评估所做的决策的可靠性。

Method: 在五个检索基准和一个TREC RAG 2025端到端设置中，将答案支持信号应用于四种角色：比较检索器、引导检索训练与系统选择、预测下游答案质量、过滤提供给生成器的证据。通过人类标注者验证过滤干预，并比较不同答案评估者的结论。

Result: 答案支持信号改变了检索排名，但在下游价值上不统一：它不能可靠改善检索器训练；对系统选择的价值取决于生成器如何使用证据；无法稳健预测未知主题的答案质量；证据过滤虽被人类标注者确认为保留有效证据，但不同答案评估者对答案质量是否改善得出不同结论。

Conclusion: 使检索评估更贴近生成所需的证据，并不能自动使所有下游评估使用更可靠。RAG评估方法应根据其旨在支持的具体比较、决策和结论来加以评估。

Abstract: Retrieval evaluation for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly designed around whether retrieved passages contain evidence that can support generation, rather than topical relevance alone. We study whether this closer alignment with downstream evidence needs also makes retrieval evaluation more useful for the decisions built from it.
  Across five retrieval benchmarks and an end-to-end TREC RAG 2025 setting, we examine an answer-support signal in four roles: comparing retrievers, guiding retrieval training and system selection, predicting downstream answer quality, and filtering the evidence supplied to a generator. The signal changes retrieval rankings, but its downstream value is not uniform. It does not reliably improve retriever training; the benefit of using it for system selection depends on how the generator is instructed to use the retrieved evidence; and retrieval scores based on it do not robustly predict answer quality on unseen topics. In a direct evidence intervention, human annotators confirm that filtering preferentially preserves passages containing useful answer evidence, yet different answer evaluators reach different conclusions about whether the resulting answers improve.
  These results show that making retrieval evaluation more closely reflect the evidence needed for generation does not by itself make every downstream use of that evaluation more reliable. RAG evaluation methods should therefore be assessed with respect to the particular comparisons, decisions, and conclusions they are intended to support.

</details>


### [2] [Preference Flow Matching with Spectral Factorization for Micro-video Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26579)
*Xinxin Dong,Haokai Ma,Fei Hu,YuZe Zheng,Bin Wu,Yonghui Yang,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PrismRec，一种基于流匹配的微视频推荐框架，通过频谱语义分解（SSF）将帧级视频表示分解为互补的静态语义和动态因子，并通过上下文校准偏好匹配（CPM）将用户敏感性校准后的上下文作为结构化条件引导生成轨迹，使视频内容成为偏好形成的内在驱动而非辅助信息。在四个数据集上最高超越SOTA基线22.65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微视频推荐方法存在两个关键缺陷：一是将帧序列压缩为单一整体表示，混淆了稳定视觉语义与动态演化特征；二是基于扩散/流匹配的推荐器仅用粗粒度行为上下文作为条件，忽略了其内部时间结构对偏好形成的作用。因此需要一种能将视频内容解耦并作为结构化条件驱动偏好形成的方法。

Method: PrismRec包含两个核心模块：1) 频谱语义分解（SSF）：类比棱镜分光，在时间频域中通过先验引导的可学习频率掩码，从帧级表示中分离出互补的静态语义因子和动态因子；2) 上下文校准偏好匹配（CPM）：根据每个用户对不同因子的敏感性进行加权，并将校准后的上下文作为结构化条件注入流匹配过程，引导匹配轨迹朝目标表示演化。

Result: 在来自两个平台的四个数据集上，PrismRec相比SOTA基线最高提升22.65%，同时在所有对比方法中具有最低的推理成本和峰值内存占用。

Conclusion: PrismRec验证了将视频内容解耦为静态与动态因子、并以校准上下文作为结构化条件注入流匹配过程的有效性，为微视频推荐提供了一种以内容为内在驱动的偏好生成新范式，同时兼顾了性能与计算效率。

Abstract: Micro-video recommendation aims to infer user preferences from historical interactions and multimodal video content, thereby identifying the next video of interest. However, prevailing methods compress frame sequences into a single holistic representation, entangling the stable visual semantics and the evolving dynamics that jointly shape user preferences. Meanwhile, diffusion- and flow matching-based recommenders condition their generation process solely on coarse behavioral context, leaving its internal temporal structure outside preference formation. We therefore propose PrismRec, a Preference Flow Matching framework with Spectral Factorization for Micro-video Recommendation. Analogous to a prism that disperses white light into its constituent spectrum, PrismRec devises Spectral Semantic Factorization (SSF) to derive complementary static semantic and dynamic factors from frame-level representations via a prior-guided learnable frequency mask in the temporal frequency domain. Then, it proposes Context-Calibrated Preference Matching (CPM) to weigh them with each user's specific sensitivity and inject the calibrated context as a structured condition to steer the matching trajectory toward the target representation, making video content as an intrinsic driver of preference formation rather than auxiliary side information. Experiments on four datasets from two platforms show that PrismRec surpasses the SOTA baseline by up to 22.65%, with the lowest inference cost and peak memory among the compared methods.

</details>


### [3] [hoBIT: A Profile-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Chatbot for University Academic Advising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26604)
*Yoonseo Kim,Seongmin Lee,Joongheon Kim,SeongKu Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: proFILL 是一种将高校现有规则型咨询聊天机器人 hoBIT 改造为"画像感知"的检索增强生成（RAG）系统的方法。它通过渐进式获取每个查询所需的画像属性（如院系、入学批次、学位项目），并用这些属性对检索结果进行条件化处理，从而避免了"无画像检索器"返回看似合理但不适用证据的问题。实验和人工偏好研究证明其优于多种 RAG 基线，并支持开源模型以实现低成本本地部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大学学术咨询场景中，相同的问题可能因学生的院系、入学批次和学位项目不同而需要不同的回答。现有的"画像盲"（profile-blind）检索器会返回看似合理但实际上不适用于该学生的证据，导致回答错误。这一核心问题驱动了 proFILL 的设计。

Method: proFILL 将规则型聊天机器人 hoBIT 转化为画像感知的 RAG 系统。其关键机制是：不要求用户一开始就提供完整画像，而是根据查询意图和初次检索到的证据，逐步只获取当前查询所需的画像属性，并用这些属性对基于画像感知索引的检索进行条件化处理。

Result: 大量实验和人工偏好研究表明，proFILL 在多种 RAG 基线上表现出更优的性能，被目标用户更偏好选择，并且在使用开源（open-weight）模型时依然有效，能够支持成本效益高的本地部署。

Conclusion: proFILL 证明了一种可行的路径：通过渐进式画像感知检索，可以在不苛求用户完整画像的前提下，显著提升大学学术咨询场景中检索回答的准确性与适用性，且具备实际部署的经济可行性。

Abstract: In university academic advising, identical questions can require different answers depending on a student's department, admission cohort, and degree program, causing profile-blind retrievers to surface plausible but inapplicable evidence. We present proFILL, a method for transforming hoBIT, our college's current rule-based advising chatbot, into a profile-aware retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system. Rather than requiring a complete user profile upfront, proFILL progressively acquires only the profile attributes needed for each query, guided by both the query intent and the initially retrieved evidence, and uses them to condition retrieval over a profile-aware index. Extensive experiments and a human preference study show that proFILL outperforms diverse RAG baselines, is preferred by target users, and remains effective with open-weight models for cost-effective on-premise deployment.

</details>


### [4] [When Does Supervised Fine-Tuning Reduce Instruction Sensitivity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26661)
*Jaekeol Choi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了监督微调（SFT）对大型语言模型指令敏感性的影响。通过固定模型检查点在多种改写指令下评估性能，并以性能标准差定义指令敏感性，研究发现SFT并不统一降低指令敏感性：在小模型（1.7B和4B）上SFT显著降低敏感性（约54-71%），而在8B模型上效果不显著；跨模型（Gemma-2-9B vs Mistral-7B）和跨评估协议（自由生成 vs 似然强制选择）下结论存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型对同一任务的不同指令表述会表现出显著的性能波动，但此前研究尚未明确传统的任务特定监督微调（SFT）如何改变这种指令敏感性。理解这一点对于开发稳健的指令跟随模型至关重要。

Method: 作者在固定模型检查点下用多种改写指令进行评估，将指令敏感性定义为各指令下任务性能的标准差。研究采用受控规模分析（Qwen3 1.7B/4B/8B，MS MARCO数据集），并辅以跨模型族验证（Mistral-7B和Gemma-2-9B）。使用查询级bootstrap分析检验统计显著性，并在ESCI-English数据集上对比了自由生成和基于似然的强制选择两种评估协议。

Result: SFT前，指令敏感性随Qwen3模型规模增大而显著下降。1.7B和4B模型上SFT一致降低训练指令下的敏感性（约54-71%）。8B模型上个体敏感性变化统计上不显著，但训练指令间的配对对比在查询级bootstrap下统计可靠且三个随机种子方向一致。Gemma-2-9B表现出与Qwen3-8B相同的方向性训练指令对比，而Mistral-7B没有。ESCI-English实验显示不同评估协议可能得出定性不同的稳健性结论。

Conclusion: SFT并非统一降低指令敏感性：其稳健性效果取决于适配设置（模型规模），而测量到的敏感性还取决于预测和评分协议。这提示在评估模型指令稳健性时需考虑模型规模、模型族和评估协议等多个维度。

Abstract: Large language models can exhibit substantial performance variation across alternative formulations of the same task instruction, yet it remains unclear how conventional task-specific supervised fine-tuning (SFT) changes this instruction sensitivity. We study this question by evaluating fixed model checkpoints under multiple paraphrased instructions and defining instruction sensitivity as the standard deviation of task performance across them. We conduct a controlled scale analysis with Qwen3 models at 1.7B, 4B, and 8B on MS MARCO, together with targeted cross-family checks using Mistral-7B and Gemma-2-9B. Before SFT, instruction sensitivity decreases sharply with Qwen3 model scale. At 1.7B and 4B, SFT consistently reduces sensitivity across training instructions, with reductions of approximately 54--71%. At 8B, individual sensitivity changes are not statistically distinguishable from zero, but paired contrasts between training instructions are statistically reliable under query-level bootstrap analysis and have consistent directions across all three random seeds. Gemma-2-9B shows the same directional training-instruction contrast as Qwen3-8B, whereas Mistral-7B does not, suggesting that the strength of this effect also varies across models. Experiments on ESCI-English further show that free-generation and likelihood-based forced-choice evaluation can yield qualitatively different robustness conclusions even when valid-label generation is nearly perfect and average task performance is similar. Overall, SFT does not uniformly reduce instruction sensitivity: its robustness effect depends on the adaptation setting, while measured sensitivity can additionally depend on the prediction and scoring protocol.

</details>


### [5] [BLANC: Discovering Patent White Space via Changes in Normalized Pointwise Mutual Information Between Multi-View Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26685)
*Shuichi Miyazawa,Kensuke Fujii*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出BLANC（空白图谱分析）三阶段流水线，通过多视角神经主题建模、NPMI量化跨维度簇关联及条件检测，自动识别专利图谱中"全局已建立但局部未探索"的技术空白区域，并在两个USPTO公开语料及一个工业案例中验证其高特异性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利空白空间（未探索但潜在有价值的区域）的识别对战略研发规划至关重要，但现有方法依赖人工专利映射或采用单视角聚类，缺乏定量间隙检测能力，难以自动化、客观地发现空白组合。

Method: 提出BLANC三阶段流水线：(1) 沿三个语义维度（应用/用途、新颖性、创造性步骤）进行多视角神经主题建模；(2) 用NPMI量化跨维度主题簇之间的关联强度；(3) 条件检测——当语料被用户指定关键词过滤后，NPMI下降的组合被标记为空白，下降幅度由新指标ΔNPMI衡量，识别"全局已建立、局部未探索"的组合。

Result: 在两个USPTO公开语料（ML/AI 5,417篇CPC G06N；玻璃成分1,982篇CPC C03C）上，通过人为耗尽已知技术组合后测试恢复率：目标组合75%文档被移除后恢复率达34.1%（ML/AI）和27.3%（玻璃），而191次诱饵试验（随机删除或删除其他既有组合）中目标从未被恢复；将三个语义视角合并为单视角则无法恢复任何组合，先验共现度量在随机删除下会误标记目标，缺乏特异性。工业案例（302篇浮法玻璃/玻璃微晶专利）中，关键词"fluorine"检测出"氟表面处理×翘曲抑制"候选组合（ΔNPMI最高0.48），与专家独立发现的结论一致。

Conclusion: BLANC通过多视角建模与条件NPMI下降检测，实现了对专利图谱空白空间的高特异性自动识别，显著优于单视角聚类和先验共现度量，并已在工业案例中得到专家验证，为战略R&D规划提供了可行的定量工具。

Abstract: Identifying white space --- the unexplored but potentially valuable regions of a patent landscape --- is essential for strategic R&D planning, yet existing methods rely on manual patent mapping or apply single-view clustering without quantitative gap detection. We propose BLANC (Blank Landscape Analysis through NPMI Conditioning), a three-phase pipeline combining (1) multi-view neural topic modeling along three semantic dimensions (application/use, novelty, inventive step); (2) Normalized Pointwise Mutual Information (NPMI) to quantify cross-dimensional cluster association; and (3) conditional detection that flags combinations whose NPMI drops when the corpus is filtered by a user-specified keyword. The drop is captured by a new metric, $Δ$NPMI, which identifies combinations "established globally, unexplored locally." Because white space has no ground truth, we evaluate BLANC on two public USPTO corpora --- machine learning/AI (5,417 patents, CPC G06N) and glass compositions (1,982 patents, CPC C03C) --- by artificially depleting known technology combinations and testing recovery. When three-quarters of a target pair's documents are removed, BLANC recovers 34.1% (ML/AI) and 27.3% (glass) of the depleted combinations, whereas size-matched removals not aimed at them (random documents, or those of a different established combination) essentially never do: the target is never recovered in 191 decoy trials. Collapsing the three semantic views into one recovers nothing, while prior co-occurrence measures also flag the target under random removal, offering no specificity. In a proprietary case (302 float glass / glass-ceramics patents), the keyword "fluorine" reveals a fluorine surface treatment $\times$ warpage suppression candidate ($Δ$NPMI up to 0.48) that experts had independently identified.

</details>


### [6] [Beyond a Single Story: Meta-Reviewing Sparse and Incomplete User-generated Contents for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26728)
*Hongren Wang,Tianjun Wei,Yingpeng Du,Jie Zhang,Yin-Leng Theng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MOSAIC框架，通过聚合邻居用户评论中的属性-情感证据构建"元评论"，缓解推荐系统中用户生成内容（UGC）稀疏和不完整的问题，并采用多门控专家混合（MMoE）架构联合优化评分预测和元评论属性-情感预测，同时通过注意力模块个性化聚合信号，提升推荐准确率和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中数据稀疏是长期挑战，而依赖用户生成内容（如文本评论）的方法更为严重：UGC存在评论缺失（交互无评论）和评论不完整（仅覆盖部分属性）的问题，现有方法常忽略这些UGC特有缺陷，导致推荐精度下降。

Method: MOSAIC借鉴学术同行评审中的"meta-review"概念，为每个目标用户从邻居用户评论中聚合属性-情感证据构建元评论；采用多门控混合专家（MMoE）架构联合优化评分预测与元评论属性-情感预测；注意力模块将聚合的元评论信号个性化到每个目标用户，同时生成精化的评分预测和属性级解释。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，MOSAIC在推荐准确率和解释质量上均一致优于最先进的基线方法，有效缓解UGC稀疏与不完整问题，且对交互历史有限的用户也能带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: MOSAIC通过元评论聚合策略和MMoE多任务学习架构，在缓解UGC稀疏与不完整性的同时实现了推荐准确性与可解释性的双重提升，验证了借用学术评审中元评论思想解决推荐系统数据稀疏问题的有效性。

Abstract: Data sparsity remains a long-standing challenge in recommender systems, and it becomes more severe for methods relying on user-generated content (UGC) such as textual reviews, which capture fine-grained preferences but require more user efforts to produce. As a result, UGC exhibits (1) missing reviews, where interactions lack any review, and (2) incomplete reviews, where available reviews cover only a subset of relevant attributes. Existing approaches often overlook these UGC-specific issues, leading to degraded accuracy. Motivated by meta-review in academic peer review, we propose MOSAIC (Meta-review On Sparse And Incomplete user-generated Content), which constructs a meta-review for each target user by aggregating attribute-sentiment evidence from neighbor users' reviews. A multi-gate mixture-of-experts (MMoE) architecture jointly optimizes rating prediction and meta-review attribute-sentiment prediction, while an attention module personalizes the aggregated meta-review signals to each target user, yielding both refined rating predictions and attribute-level explanations. Experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that MOSAIC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both recommendation accuracy and explanation quality, mitigating UGC sparsity and incompleteness while delivering consistent gains for users with limited interaction history.

</details>


### [7] [STREAM: An Objective-Driven and Uncertainty-Aware Framework for Industrial Energy Data Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26754)
*Zhipeng Ma,Bo Nørregaard Jørgensen,Zheng Grace Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出STREAM框架，一个面向目标的、考虑不确定性的工业能源数据采集工作流，将客观的数据连通性评估扩展到"数据是否适用于能源绩效评估"的分析适用性验证，并通过两个工业批量生产案例（铸造感应炉熔炼和奶酪粉干燥）验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工业能源数据采集工作流主要关注连通性和存储，而忽略了对采集信号是否满足能源绩效评估需求的验证。数据可访问并不等于数据分析适用，缺乏一种系统化的方法确保采集到的数据真正服务于定义的能源绩效评估目标。

Method: STREAM框架包含六个阶段：目标规范、技术要求、资源映射、来源提取、档案元数据和数据库迁移。框架在原有概念序列基础上增加了阶段级工件、最小证据门槛、来源适用性规则、元数据模板、不确定性量规和特定案例的可追溯性矩阵，并在所有六个阶段中评估测量、时间、上下文和处理四类不确定性。

Result: 通过两个工业批量生产案例（铸造厂感应炉熔炼和使用SCADA与生产订单数据的奶酪粉干燥）的验证，结果表明数据可访问性与分析适用性不等同，STREAM能够支持关于即时数据使用、分析限制和优先基础设施改进的透明决策。

Conclusion: STREAM框架成功地将工业能源数据工作流从"能否获取数据"导向"数据是否合用"的目标驱动范式，提供了从目标到数据的端到端可追溯性，为工业能源管理中的数据质量验证和基础设施投资决策提供了结构化支持。

Abstract: Industrial energy management requires datasets that connect energy use with equipment states, production batches, material flows, and process conditions. However, conventional acquisition workflows commonly emphasize connectivity and storage without verifying whether accessible signals satisfy the requirements of a defined energy-performance assessment. This paper presents STREAM, an objective-driven and uncertainty-aware framework comprising Specification of Objectives, Technical Requirements, Resource Mapping, Extraction from Sources, Archival Metadata, and Migration to Database. STREAM is the central workflow: objective-to-data traceability is its end-to-end output, while measurement, temporal, contextual, and processing uncertainty are assessed across all six stages. Compared with the original conceptual STREAM sequence, this paper adds stage-level artifacts, minimum-evidence gates, source-suitability rules, a metadata template, an uncertainty rubric, and case-specific traceability matrices. The framework is validated through two industrial batch-process cases: induction-furnace melting in a foundry and cheese-powder drying using SCADA and production-order data. The results demonstrate that data accessibility is not equivalent to analytical suitability and show how STREAM supports transparent decisions about immediate data use, analytical restrictions, and prioritized infrastructure improvements.

</details>


### [8] [When Memory Takes Gradients: Collaborative Vector Memory for Agentic Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26895)
*Hanchong Chen,Xing Tang,Lingjie Li,Xiongfeng Shan,Xiuqiang He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CoVeMem（协同向量记忆），将智能体推荐系统中LLM的文本记忆向量化为基于冻结LightGCN用户/物品状态的记忆库，通过对比对齐和列表式协同训练，在4个基准的20个指标单元中19个达到或超越最强文本记忆智能体，且无需额外LLM调用维护记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体推荐系统以文本形式维护用户记忆，存在两大局限：一是文本记忆一次只能重写一次，无法高效利用完整交互历史；二是协同证据（整个目录上的渐变相似度）无法在翻译成句子后得到保留，限制了推荐质量。

Method: CoVeMem将智能体记忆的协同核心向量化：用冻结的LightGCN用户和物品状态构成记忆库；每次决策时候选集自身检索最相关的历史状态，作为软token连同轻量文本画像进入LLM上下文；通过对比对齐到物品语义锚点，再利用带掩码候选的列表式协同训练，教会模型阅读这些状态并通过它们排序；最后用逐点yes/no读数给每个候选打分。

Result: 在四个指令化推荐基准上，CoVeMem在20个指标单元中有19个达到或超越最强协同文本记忆智能体，且记忆维护所需额外LLM调用为零（仅共享静态画像），而文本记忆需每次交互调用。

Conclusion: 记忆现在可以接收梯度：完整交互历史（文本方式无法触及）可被用作训练数据，同时训练智能体记住什么以及如何解读所记住的内容。

Abstract: Agentic recommender systems ground each decision of a large language model (LLM) in a persistent memory of the user, and in existing agents that memory is text: a narrative written and maintained by further LLM calls. Text limits this memory in two ways. It is updated one rewrite at a time, so exploiting the full interaction history is prohibitively expensive; and collaborative evidence, graded similarity over an entire catalog, does not survive translation into sentences. We propose CoVeMem (Collaborative Vector Memory), which vectorizes the collaborative core of the agent's memory. Frozen LightGCN user and item states form the memory bank; at each decision, the candidate set itself retrieves the most relevant historical states, which enter the LLM's context as soft tokens alongside a light textual profile. Contrastive alignment to item-semantic anchors, followed by listwise co-training with masked candidates, teaches the model to read these states and to rank through them; a pointwise yes/no readout scores each candidate. Across four instruction-grounded recommendation benchmarks, CoVeMem matches or exceeds the strongest collaborative text-memory agent on 19 of 20 metric cells while requiring zero additional LLM calls for memory maintenance beyond the shared static profile, against per-interaction calls for text memory. The memory now takes gradients: the full interaction history, out of reach for text, becomes available as training data for what the agent remembers and for how it reads what it remembers.

</details>


### [9] [Topology-Masked Unified Backbone for Joint Feature Interaction and Multi-Domain Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27005)
*Zhihao Zhu,Dezheng Han,Jikang Xia,Shuaishuai Guo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了MaskRec，一种拓扑掩码统一令牌交互架构，在统一令牌空间中同时完成特征交互与多域序列建模，通过结构化注意力掩码（TopoMask）和双路径交互查询生成模块提升工业级CVR预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业排序模型通常将异构特征交互和多域用户行为序列建模作为两个独立模块分别处理。虽然近期有统一架构尝试将二者整合到单一框架中，但这种统一往往依赖于模块间的协调配合，并未将所有信息源真正组织在同一个交互空间内，限制了信息充分交互的能力。

Method: MaskRec包含三个核心设计：(1) 将异构特征、多域行为序列和上下文信号转化为统一令牌表示，并引入可学习的全局记忆令牌和域级记忆令牌作为信息聚合节点；(2) 设计结构化的注意力掩码TopoMask，根据不同信息源的结构差异和建模需求，选择性地启用或阻断注意力连接，使特征交互和多域序列建模在同一拓扑约束的注意力过程中完成；(3) 设计双路径交互查询生成模块，在进入统一主干前注入候选条件化的用户-物品交互信号。

Result: 在腾讯广告算法大赛数据集上，MaskRec相比官方基线取得了稳定的改进，验证了该统一框架用于工业级CVR预测的有效性。

Conclusion: MaskRec通过将多种信息源统一到令牌空间并利用拓扑约束的注意力机制，在无需模块间协调的前提下实现了特征交互与多域序列建模的真正统一，为工业大规模CVR预测提供了一种有效的统一架构方案。

Abstract: Large-scale post-click conversion rate (CVR) prediction requires jointly modeling heterogeneous feature interactions and dependencies over multi-domain user behavior sequences. Existing industrial ranking models usually handle these two aspects with separate modules. Recent unified architectures attempt to incorporate them into a single framework, but such unification often relies on coordination between modules and does not fully organize all information sources within the same interaction space. To address this problem, we propose MaskRec, a topology-masked unified token interaction architecture for feature interaction and multi-domain sequence modeling. MaskRec transforms heterogeneous features, multi-domain behavior sequences, and contextual signals into unified token representations, and further introduces learnable global memory tokens and domain-level memory tokens as information aggregation nodes. Based on this unified token space, MaskRec designs a structured attention mask, TopoMask, which selectively enables or blocks attention connections according to the structural differences and modeling requirements of different information sources. In this way, heterogeneous feature interaction and multi-domain sequence modeling are performed within the same topology-constrained attention process. In addition, MaskRec incorporates a dual-path interactive query generation module to inject candidate-conditioned user--item interaction signals before the unified backbone. Experiments on the Tencent Advertising Algorithm Competition dataset show that MaskRec achieves stable improvements over the official baseline, validating the effectiveness of the proposed unified framework for industrial CVR prediction.

</details>


### [10] [Conversational Recommendation over Live E-Commerce Catalogues with Self-Refreshing Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27006)
*Ante Kapetanovic,Tomislav Duricic,Dionizije Fa,Andro Mercep,Emanuel Lacic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一个面向实时商品目录的多轮对话购物助手，其核心是自刷新检索器，通过哈希增量同步商品变化，仅用LLM做意图分类与偏好挖掘，检索、重排和多样性选择由专用函数完成，并在WhatsApp上进行了演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的对话推荐系统通常在静态、预索引的商品集合上评估，但电商目录是动态变化的（商品新增、删除、调价、补货等），静态评估无法反映真实场景，需要一个能够处理实时目录变化的对话购物助手。

Method: 提出一个商家无关的多轮对话购物助手，核心组件为自刷新检索引擎：摄入商家商品feed、富化记录并同步至向量索引；每次运行时通过商品哈希识别新增、变更、删除和未变化的商品，仅处理增量而非全量重建。控制器驱动的对话层使用该索引，LLM仅用于意图分类和偏好挖掘，检索、重排和多样性选择由专用函数完成。

Result: 在WhatsApp上部署了演示购物助手，目录变更在下一次成功同步后即反映到推荐结果中，并提供线上聊天机器人、文档和录屏演示。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了如何在实时动态电商目录上构建高效的LLM对话推荐系统，通过增量同步机制避免全量索引重建，并验证了其在真实通讯平台上的可行性。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems based on large language models (LLMs) are usually evaluated on static, pre-indexed item collections, yet e-commerce catalogues change continuously as products are added or removed, repriced, and restocked. We present a merchant-agnostic, multi-turn conversational shopping assistant that operates over such live catalogues. Its central component is a self-refreshing retriever that ingests a merchant product feed, enriches the records, and synchronizes them into a vector index. On each run, per-item hashes identify which products are new, changed, deleted, or unchanged, so only the delta is processed rather than rebuilding the whole catalogue. A controller-based dialogue layer consumes this index, using an LLM only for intent classification and preference elicitation while retrieval, reranking, and diversity selection run as dedicated functions. Our demonstration is a WhatsApp shopping assistant in which catalogue changes reach the recommendations after the next successful sync. A live chatbot, documentation, and a recorded walkthrough are available at https://github.com/infobip/infobip-agentic-crs.

</details>


### [11] [ProRetrieval: Learning to Orchestrate Hybrid Search via Executable Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27017)
*Chengsong You,Zhen Sun,Yunhai Hu,Junwei Zhou,Xiaoyu Cao,Binyu Li,Ziyan Zhao,Weiyao Wang,Liren Lu,Zhijie Ye,Yumo Cao,Yitao Long,Yiwei Xu,Qiyi Jiang,Xuanyi Fu,Yufan Chen,Yilun Li,Rongkang Xiong,Yiran Zou,Nan Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ProRetrieval，将语言模型重构为检索编排器，把自然语言查询转化为混合DSL程序，通过SQL逻辑代数融合结构化字段与文本/图像向量检索的异构候选集，并用GRPO和DAPO训练Qwen3-4B，在两个新基准上超越了GPT-5.5等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实检索常需将结构化约束与跨文本/图像的语义意图通过任意布尔逻辑组合。现有混合流程（如倒数排名融合、自查询检索器）仅支持固定组合形式，而近期基于强化学习的检索器将LM仅训练为单后端的查询生成器，异构检索路径的编排超出了其动作空间。

Method: ProRetrieval将LM重定位为检索编排器：给定自然语言查询，合成一个混合DSL可执行程序，该DSL在结构化字段上交错SQL操作符，并对文本和图像使用向量检索原语，其中SQL本身提供融合异构候选集的逻辑代数。使用GRPO和DAPO算法在分层四项奖励下训练Qwen3-4B模型。

Result: 在基于Amazon产品和Enron邮件构建的两个新基准上，ProRetrieval的4B模型在Hit@1指标上超越GPT-5.5（电商领域0.81 vs 0.69；邮件领域0.91 vs 0.86），同时优于Claude Opus 4.7以及检索、LLM增强、结构化查询和图基基线的综合套件。

Conclusion: ProRetrieval证明通过将LM训练为可编排任意异构检索路径的检索编排器，可以显著超越超大规模通用模型和既有混合检索基线，为复杂组合检索提供更灵活、更强大的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world retrieval often composes structured constraints with semantic intents over text and images through arbitrary Boolean logic. Existing hybrid pipelines such as reciprocal rank fusion or self-querying retrievers admit only a fixed form of composition, while recent reinforcement-learning retrievers train the language model as a query generator for a single backend, leaving the orchestration of heterogeneous retrieval paths outside its action space. We propose ProRetrieval, which recasts the language model as a retrieval orchestrator: given a natural-language query, it synthesizes an executable program in a hybrid DSL interleaving SQL operators over structured fields with vector-retrieval primitives over text and images, with SQL itself providing the logical algebra that fuses heterogeneous candidate sets. We train Qwen3-4B with GRPO and DAPO under a hierarchical four-term reward, and evaluate on two new benchmarks built from Amazon products and Enron email. Our 4B model surpasses GPT-5.5 (Hit@1 0.81 vs. 0.69 on e-commerce; 0.91 vs. 0.86 on email) and Claude Opus 4.7 and a comprehensive suite of retrieval, LLM-augmented, structured-query, and graph-based baselines. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProRetrieval/; data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymous-7219/ProRetrieval.

</details>


### [12] [Astar: Learning to Propose Evolution Directions for Self-Evolving Industrial AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27287)
*Jinxin Hu,Hao Deng,Haibo Xing,Lingyu Mu,Muyu Zou,Weiqin Yang,Sirui Chen,Bohao Wang,Zhezheng Hao,Hao Zhang,Zulong Chen,Shizhun Wang,Yu Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Astar，一种基于训练的进化方向引导模型，从工业系统的海量迭代历史中学习如何提出有效的AI进化方向，解决了传统上依赖资深专家的人工瓶颈。Astar-8B在真实执行评估中单次建议成功率远超人类专家和最强通用大模型，并已部署于阿里巴巴Lazada广告系统，实现了全自动迭代闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统通过持续迭代演进，其中大部分阶段已自动化，但"提出有效进化方向"这一起点仍然是严重依赖资深专家的关键瓶颈。通用LLM（即使是最先进的GPT-5.5）只能提供泛泛且错位的建议，因为该领域专业知识是通过经验积累而非显式编码的，难以直接注入模型。

Method: Astar采用基于训练的方法（而非提示工程），从工业系统丰富的迭代历史中学习专门的进化引导模型。针对四个挑战（稀疏监督、噪声数据、广阔方向空间、昂贵的验证成本），在数据侧设计流水线将噪声历史提交转换为大规模清洁语料（成对样本扩展+噪声过滤）；在模型侧通过中期训练、SFT和RL训练模型，用层级提示引导进化方向生成，并用奖励模型作为RL中的快速替代评估器。

Result: Astar已部署于阿里巴巴Lazada广告系统的进化方向提议中。Astar-8B单次建议成功率0.6786，远超人类专家(0.3229)和最强通用LLM(0.3071)。在两周内引导20次连续迭代，离线Hitrate@200提升23.6%，在线A/B测试中GMV相对提升4.86%，广告收入提升1.82%。

Conclusion: Astar验证了AI可以替代人类专家承担提出进化方向这一关键角色，通过从工业级迭代历史中学习而非依赖显式编码知识，成功实现了AI系统全自动迭代闭环，在真实生产环境中取得了显著且可验证的业务收益。

Abstract: Modern AI systems advance through continuous iteration: a loop of proposing evolution directions, implementing code, training, and evaluation. While the latter three stages are increasingly automated, the starting point --- proposing effective evolution directions --- remains a critical bottleneck that still relies heavily on senior experts. In this work, we explore whether AI can take over this role. We find that general-purpose LLMs, even the advanced GPT-5.5, offer only generic and misaligned suggestions: the required expertise is accumulated through experience rather than explicitly codified, and thus hard to inject directly.
  To this end, we propose Astar, a training-based approach that learns a specialized evolution-guiding model from the abundant iteration histories of industrial systems. Realizing this idea, however, raises four challenges: sparse supervision, noisy data, a vast direction space, and prohibitively expensive verification. We address them along two fronts. On the data side, we design a pipeline that turns noisy historical commits into a large, clean evolutionary corpus via pairwise sample expansion and noise filtering. On the model side, we train the model through mid-training, SFT, and RL, guiding evolution direction generation with hierarchical hints and using the reward model in RL as a fast surrogate evaluator.
  Astar has been deployed in Alibaba's Lazada advertising system for evolution direction proposal. Astar-8B achieves a single-proposal success rate of 0.6786 in real-execution evaluation, far exceeding human experts (0.3229) and the strongest general-purpose LLM (0.3071). More importantly, Astar closes the loop and enables fully automatic iteration: it guided 20 consecutive iterations over two weeks, improving offline Hitrate@200 by 23.6%, while an online A/B test yielded relative lifts of 4.86% in GMV and 1.82% in advertising revenue.

</details>


### [13] [Stageboost: Recommending Signals Based on Counterfactual Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27366)
*Darpan Singhal,Matan Mandelbrod,Tal Franji,Manasa Kolla,Vipul Gaba,Yuri Brovman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了eBay使用的一种基于两阶段XGBoost的模型，用于在商品详情页（VI页）上最优地展示信号（短文本或视觉片段），以提供上下文信息、促进智能购买并提升用户参与度。该模型在在线实验中使整体GMB（总购买商品额）提升0.08%，其中配件类目GMB提升0.58%，主要归因于高均价商品的转化率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eBay的VI页面需要展示信号以向用户提供额外的上下文信息，从而促进智能购买决策并激励用户参与。关键在于如何从大量可用信号中选择和放置最优的信号组合，以最大化业务指标（如GMB）。

Method: 提出了一种两阶段XGBoost模型的解决方案：第一阶段可能负责候选信号的筛选或排序，第二阶段进一步优化信号的最终选择和展示位置，从而使VI页面上的信号填充达到最优配置。该方法基于梯度提升树（XGBoost）框架构建。

Result: 在线实验结果显示，该模型使整体GMB提升了0.08%，配件类目（Parts and Accessories）GMB提升了0.58%。增益主要来自高平均价格商品转化率的提升。

Conclusion: 两阶段XGBoost模型能够有效地解决VI页面信号的最优填充问题，实现在线业务指标（GMB）的正向提升，尤其在配件类目和高单价商品场景下效果显著。

Abstract: Signals are short textual or visual snippets displayed on the eBay View-Item (VI) page, providing additional, contextual information for users about the viewed item. The aim of displaying these signals is to facilitate intelligent purchase and to incentivize engagement. In this paper, we present a 2 stage xgboost based model that optimally populates the VI page with signals. This approach has shown a 0.08% lift in overall GMB (Gross Merchandise Bought) and 0.58% increase in Parts and Accessories GMB, primarily due to increase in conversion of high average price items in online experimentation.

</details>


### [14] [Scaling Graph Neural Networks for Friend Recommendation: Multi-Hash User Embeddings and Temporal Neighbor Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27413)
*Maksim Utushkin,Andrei Ovsiannikov,Alexander D'yakonov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向生产规模社交图谱的可扩展端到端GNN排序系统，通过多哈希ID嵌入和基于时间戳的邻域采样两大核心设计，在1.94亿用户和280亿条边的图上将ID嵌入表大小缩减98%以上，并将时序采样复杂度从O(deg(v)+k)降至O(log(deg(v))+k)，在线A/B测试中好友推荐增加16%、独立好友添加用户数增加11.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 好友推荐本质上具有图结构特性，多跳社交上下文比用户属性更能决定潜在连接的关联性。然而在生产规模社交图谱（数亿用户、数百亿边）上部署消息传递GNN面临严峻的建模与系统挑战，尤其是在高基数列ID嵌入表过大（超过200GB）以及面向拥有数万好友的用户进行时序邻居采样时全邻接表扫描不可行。

Method: 系统包含两个关键设计选择：1) 多哈希ID嵌入作为主要节点表征，将ID嵌入表大小减少98%以上同时保持排序质量；2) 时间戳排序的CSR存储配合二分查找，将每节点时序采样成本从O(deg(v)+k)优化至O(log(deg(v))+k)。此外还发布了用于大规模时序图分布式训练与推理的框架。

Result: 在包含1.94亿用户和280亿条边的图谱上，离线消融实验分别隔离验证了各设计选择的贡献；在线A/B测试中相比强生产基线，好友推荐带来的好友添加量提升16%，独立好友添加用户数提升11.5%。

Conclusion: 多哈希ID嵌入与时间戳排序CSR加二分查找的时序邻居采样组合在扩展性、效率和在线业务指标上均取得了显著成效，证明该方案可适用于生产规模社交图谱，并发布了相关分布式训练与推理框架以供社区使用。

Abstract: Friend recommendation is inherently graph-structured: the relevance of a potential connection depends on multi-hop social context rather than user attributes alone. However, deploying message-passing GNNs on a production-scale social graph with hundreds of millions of users and tens of billions of edges requires addressing numerous modeling and systems challenges. We present a scalable end-to-end GNN ranking system for production social graphs, focusing on two design choices that are critical in this setting: multi-hash ID embeddings and temporal neighbor sampling. Multi-hash embeddings are common for high-cardinality features, but industrial GNN systems typically either ignore trainable IDs or accept full embedding tables, exceeding 200 GB for our graph. We integrate multi-hash as the primary node representation, reducing the ID-embedding table size by more than 98 percent while preserving ranking quality. Temporal neighbor sampling is well understood in principle, but existing implementations scan full adjacency lists, which is a non-starter for users with tens of thousands of friends. We implement timestamp-sorted CSR storage with binary search, reducing the per-node temporal sampling cost from $O(deg(v) + k)$ to $O(\log(deg(v)) + k)$. Beyond these components, we show that this combination scales and yields measurable production impact. On a graph with 194M users and 28B edges, offline ablations isolate each design choice's contribution. In an online A/B test, our system increases friend additions from recommendations by 16 percent and unique friend adders by 11.5 percent over a strong production baseline. We release our framework for distributed training and inference on large temporal graphs.

</details>


### [15] [misi: a Metric Inverted Sample Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27422)
*Edgar Chavez*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出misi，一种面向一般度量空间的近似最近邻（ANN）搜索倒排索引。其核心思想是将NAPP索引从常数个枢轴推广到线性大小（与n成比例）的词汇表，每个对象由其最近的k_b个采样点表示。该设计在保持构造高效、确定性、可流式处理等结构优势的同时，将任何高召回率索引组合为更大规模数据的索引，并提供了概率模型下的召回保证与匹配极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似最近邻索引方法在构造速度、内存占用、确定性和黑盒度量支持等方面存在权衡。NAPP索引使用常数个枢轴，扩展性受限；而图的构建成本高、确定性差。作者希望设计一种结构上具备可扩展性、构造高效且可组合的索引，适用于频繁重建语料库、批量相似度计算和受限内存服务等场景。

Method: misi的核心方法包括：(1) 从数据库中随机采样规模为αn（与n成正比）的对象作为词汇表；(2) 使用可插拔的内部索引在采样点上为每个对象寻找其k_b个最近采样点，形成倒排列表；(3) 查询阶段通过idf加权共享邻居投票选出C个候选，再进行精确验证；(4) 构造过程为n次独立搜索，天然的并行化与确定性；(5) 提供概率模型分析召回保证，其中k_b在重叠间隙上仅需n的对数阶，验证预算由索引自身估算。

Result: 实验结果显示：构造10^8个向量在64核上仅需5250秒，比匹配召回率的图构建快3.7倍；在强制3GiB内存上限下可流式构建；便携式产品能在8GB强制预算内从NVMe服务10^8向量。缺点是查询时间开销较大：RAM中饱和图基线快6-16倍，0.99召回率的验证预算按n^0.30增长。所有结果附带种子、饱和扫描和完整配置，可复现。

Conclusion: misi的优势在于结构性：构造为n次独立搜索（可并行、确定性、流式）、内存足迹可控、对黑盒度量通用。其代价体现在查询端工作量。该索引特别适合构造成本、确定性、内存占用或黑盒度量比峰值吞吐更重要的应用场景，并且通过组合性提供了从αn规模索引到n规模索引的通用构造路径。

Abstract: We present misi, an inverted index for approximate nearest-neighbor search over general metric spaces whose vocabulary is a random sample of the database, of size proportional to $n$. Each object is represented by its $k_b$ nearest sample points, found by a pluggable inner index over the sample; queries are answered by an idf-weighted shared-neighbor vote followed by exact verification of $C$ candidates. The construction generalizes the NAPP index from a constant number of pivots to a linear-size vocabulary, which keeps posting lists at constant expected length $ρ= k_b/α$ as $n$ grows and turns the index into a combinator: any high-recall index on $αn$ points yields an index on $n$ points, for any metric. A probabilistic model gives a recall guarantee -- $k_b$ logarithmic in $n$ over the overlap gap suffices, with a verification budget the index itself estimates -- and a matching limit: the vote cannot resolve overlap differences below order $1/\sqrt{k_b}$. The design's strengths are structural: construction is $n$ independent searches -- embarrassingly parallel, deterministic, $5{,}250$ s for $10^8$ vectors on 64 cores, $3.7\times$ faster than a matched-recall graph build -- it streams under an enforced 3 GiB cap, and the portable artifact serves $10^8$ vectors from NVMe within an enforced 8 GB budget, below the working floor of the SSD-graph baseline. Its cost is query-time work: saturated graph baselines answer $6$-$16\times$ faster in RAM, and the verification budget for 0.99 recall grows as $n^{0.30}$. All results carry seeds, saturation sweeps and full configurations, are generated from run manifests, and include measured negative results. The intended applications weight construction cost, determinism, memory footprint, or black-box metrics over peak throughput: frequently rebuilt corpora, batch similarity workloads, constrained-memory serving.

</details>

### [16] [EduRiskX: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework with F-Logic Reasoning for Early Academic Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26107)
*Yu Fu,Yongqi Kang,Yong Zhao,Rongfang Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EduRiskX，一个神经符号框架，结合基于Transformer的时间预测器与F-Logic符号推理，用于在线教育学生学业风险的早期检测与可解释预测。在OULAD数据集上达到0.900准确率和0.894 F1分数，平均早期检测周数为9.32周，检测率达94.30%，优于SOTA时序模型，并提供基于规则的结构化解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线教育学业风险预测模型存在两个核心问题：(1)早期检测能力有限，难以及时干预；(2)可解释性不足，黑箱模型导致在实际教学中产生信任危机，阻碍了模型的落地应用。

Method: 提出神经符号框架EduRiskX，包含三大组件：(1)神经组件：基于时序Transformer预测器，采用时序注意力建模学生纵向活动序列，结合类别加权损失和动态周截断；(2)符号组件：基于F-Logic规则库的数据驱动专家系统，扎根于Engagement Theory和Student Integration Model等教育理论，模拟人类教育者的诊断逻辑，仅由训练数据构建；(3)融合机制：通过逻辑回归融合神经风险概率与符号置信度分数，学习各信号的相对贡献。

Result: 在OULAD数据集上，采用严格的80/10/10学生级划分，学期末（第38周）准确率达0.900，F1分数为0.894；平均早期检测周数为9.32周，检测率94.30%。相比SOTA时序模型（PatchTST、iTransformer）和常见基线（LSTM、CNN），在相同条件下取得更优的召回率和更早的风险识别。

Conclusion: EduRiskX在预测性能和可解释性之间取得良好平衡。F-Logic模块提供将预测与可观察行为模式及教育理论关联的结构化规则解释，不仅提升预测效果，更增强了模型在实际教学场景中的可信度和可采纳性。

Abstract: Predicting students' academic risk in online education is crucial for enabling timely interventions that can improve retention and learning outcomes. However, existing models often suffer from limited early detection capability and insufficient interpretability, leading to a "black-box" trust crisis that hinders their adoption in real-world pedagogical settings. To address these challenges, we propose EduRiskX, a neuro-symbolic framework that integrates a temporal Transformer-based predictor with F-Logic symbolic reasoning. The neural component models longitudinal student activity sequences using temporal attention, class-weighted loss, and dynamic weekly truncation. Acting as a data-driven expert system, an F-Logic rule base -- grounded in established educational theories (Engagement Theory and Student Integration Model) to mimic the diagnostic logic of human educators -- is constructed exclusively from the training data. The neural risk probability and the symbolic confidence score are then combined through a logistic regression-based fusion mechanism that learns the relative contribution of each signal. Experiments on the Open University Learning Analytics Dataset (OULAD) using a strict 80/10/10 student-level split show that EduRiskX achieves an accuracy of 0.900 and an F1-score of 0.894 at the end of the semester (Week 38), with an average early detection week of 9.32 and a detection rate of 94.30 percent. Compared with state-of-the-art time-series models (PatchTST, iTransformer) and common deep learning baselines (LSTM, CNN), EduRiskX yields improved recall and earlier risk identification under identical conditions. Beyond predictive performance, the F-Logic module provides structured rule-based explanations linking predictions to observable behavioral patterns and educational theories.

</details>


### [17] [Standalone LLM and a Pre-specified Agentic Pipeline for Explaining ICU Mortality Predictions: a Feasibility Study on the eICU Demo Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26109)
*Di Zhu,Chen Xie,Haoyun Zhang,Zihan Wei,Ziwei Wang,Jiazhao Shi,Ziyu Wang,Qiyang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较了独立大语言模型与四步智能体流水线在ICU死亡率预测解释中的表现。XGBoost模型AUROC为0.855，智能体流水线在消除结局泄漏、提高指南依据性和数值特异性方面优于独立LLM，但在SHAP对齐和方向一致性上不如独立LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型能准确预测ICU死亡率，但仅依赖特征归因方法难以提供临床所需的叙事性解释。LLM有望填补这一空白，多步智能体流水线通过分离数据解读、指南核查和最终解释，可能是更合理的扩展方案。本研究旨在验证这一假设。

Method: 使用eICU Demo数据集的2,353例ICU住院记录（死亡率8.1%），训练XGBoost模型。在38例分层解释子集上，对比独立LLM与四步智能体流水线的解释质量，从结局泄漏、SHAP对齐（Jaccard系数）、方向一致性、指南依据性、数值特异性和临床合理性六个维度评估。

Result: XGBoost达到AUROC 0.855（95%CI 0.796-0.906）。独立LLM产生1例显式结局泄漏，智能体流水线为0例。在14例重叠病例中，独立LLM的SHAP对齐（Jaccard 0.171 vs 0.077）和方向一致性（92.9% vs 78.6%）更高；而智能体流水线在指南依据性（0.762 vs 0.143）、数值特异性（0.236 vs 0.143）和临床合理性（0.700 vs 0.671）上更优。

Conclusion: 智能体分解可改善安全性相关的指南依据性和患者特异性细节，但在高风险风险解释场景中，应将其与基于归因的检查结合使用，以弥补其在归因对齐方面的不足。

Abstract: Machine-learning models can predict ICU mortality accurately, but feature-attribution methods alone rarely provide the clinical narrative needed for bedside use. Large language models (LLMs) may bridge this gap, and multi-step agentic pipelines are a plausible extension because they separate data interpretation, guideline checking, and final explanation. This revised feasibility study preserves the original standalone-versus-agentic comparison while making the main clinical findings more explicit. Using the retained local eICU Demo artifact set (2,353 ICU stays; 8.1\% mortality), XGBoost achieved an AUROC of 0.855 (95\% CI 0.796--0.906) and an AUPRC of 0.332 (95\% CI 0.217--0.494). On a stratified 38-case explanation subset, the standalone LLM produced 1 explanation with explicit outcome leakage, whereas the four-step agentic pipeline produced none. Among the 14 cases that overlapped with the SHAP review subset, the standalone LLM showed higher SHAP alignment (mean Jaccard 0.171 versus 0.077) and higher direction consistency (92.9\% versus 78.6\%), while the agentic pipeline showed higher guideline grounding (0.762 versus 0.143), higher value specificity (0.236 versus 0.143), and slightly higher plausibility (0.700 versus 0.671). Clinically, the results suggest that agentic decomposition may improve safety-relevant grounding and patient-specific detail, but it should be paired with attribution-based checks before use in high-stakes risk explanation.

</details>


### [18] [Large Models for Battery Prognostics and Health Management: A Review and Future Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26111)
*Jiale Liu,Huan Wang,Weicheng Wang,Rong Zhu,Qiqi Wang,Min Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了大型模型（LMs）在电池预测与健康管理（BPHM）中的应用，阐述了Transformer架构、自监督预训练等基础技术如何解决传统方法在计算效率、跨域泛化、数据依赖和可解释性方面的瓶颈，并提出了未来的研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BPHM方法（基于物理模型和任务中心深度学习）面临计算效率低、参数化困难、跨域泛化差、依赖大量带标注的run-to-failure数据以及模型可解释性不足等长期瓶颈。大型模型基于Transformer架构和自监督预训练，提供了一种变革性新范式来克服这些难题，但目前缺乏对其在BPHM领域应用的系统性综述。

Method: 本文首先阐述使能LMs的基础技术（Transformer架构、自监督学习、大规模多模态数据集、参数高效微调PEFT），然后沿四个关键维度对近期进展进行分类：缓解数据稀缺、增强泛化与鲁棒性、集成领域知识实现可解释性、实现系统级自动化。

Result: LMs在BPHM领域取得了有前景的成果，但仍面临数据可获取性、智能验证、可信度和部署可行性等方面的重大挑战。文章基于这些发现提出了面向未来研究的路线图。

Conclusion: 该综述为理解和推进LM驱动的BPHM建立了系统性框架，为研究人员和实践者开发新一代电池管理系统提供了关键见解，以实现在电池全生命周期中的安全、可靠和自主运行。

Abstract: Battery Prognostics and Health Management (BPHM) is critical for ensuring the safe, reliable, and cost-effective operation of batteries across electric vehicles, grid storage, and consumer electronics. Conventional BPHM approaches, including physics-based models and task-centric deep learning methods, face challenges in computational efficiency and parameterization, cross-domain generalization, dependence on extensive labeled run-to-failure data, and model interpretability. Recent Large Models (LMs), built upon Transformer architectures and self-supervised pre-training, offer a transformative new paradigm to overcome these long-standing bottlenecks. This review provides the first comprehensive survey of LM applications in BPHM, systematically examining how these models address challenges in the field. We begin by elucidating the foundational technologies enabling LMs, including Transformer architectures, self-supervised learning, large-scale multimodal datasets, and PEFT techniques. We then categorize recent progress along four critical dimensions: mitigating data scarcity, enhancing generalization and robustness, integrating domain knowledge for interpretability, and enabling system-level automation. Despite promising results, significant challenges remain across data accessibility, intelligence validation, trustworthiness, and deployment feasibility. To guide future research, we propose a roadmap focused on building collaborative data ecosystems, validating intelligence for industrial applications, enhancing trustworthiness with physics-informed designs, and enabling efficient on-device deployment. This review establishes a systematic approach to understand and advance LM-driven BPHM, providing researchers and practitioners with essential insights for developing next-generation battery management systems capable of safe, reliable, and autonomous operation throughout battery lifecycles.

</details>


### [19] [PICasso: An AI-Enabled Design Framework for Autonomous Optimization of Silicon Photonic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26113)
*Deepak Vungarala,Deniz Najafi,Abdulrahman Aljoudi,Zahra Ghanaatian,Navid Khoshavi,Gourav Datta,Arman Roohi,Mahdi Nikdast,Shaahin Angizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PICasso框架，通过结构化NL→YAML→GDS流水线、PDK知识注入、自动布局布线、DRC/LVS验证和SAX光子仿真，实现了从自然语言规格到可制造光子集成电路的自动化设计。同时引入PIC-Set基准（36个参数化设计任务），实验表明PICasso相比vanilla LLM生成显著提升规格满足度（结构Spec@3达92.7%，功能Spec@3达52%），并将插入损耗从4.98 dB优化至3.25 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子集成电路（PIC）的设计高度依赖手动GUI工作流和专业领域知识，门槛高且效率低。虽然大语言模型（LLMs）在生成代码和电路方面展现出潜力，但直接生成PIC版图往往缺少物理合理性、不可制造且无法满足严格的设计规则。现有评估缺乏统一的基准和度量标准，亟需一个将结构化领域约束、物理验证和仿真反馈引入LLM驱动设计的系统性框架。

Method: PICasso采用多阶段流水线架构：（1）NL→YAML结构化生成，将自然语言规格转换为中间表示；（2）PDK感知知识注入，确保工艺设计套件兼容性；（3）自动化布局布线实现物理版图生成；（4）DRC/LVS验证保障设计规则和电路一致性；（5）SAX光子仿真提供功能验证和优化反馈。同时建立PIC-Set基准，涵盖36个参数化PIC设计任务，并提出结构/功能Spec@k、优化效率和扰动鲁棒性等新评估指标，在统一协议下对多个SOTA LLM进行评测。

Result: 在PIC-Set基准上，PICasso相比vanilla LLM生成显著提升端到端规格满足度。高复杂度电路上结构Spec@3达到92.7%，功能Spec@3达到52%。通过仿真引导优化，电路插入损耗从平均4.98 dB降至3.25 dB，实现了1.74 dB的改进。运行时间与手动GUI工作流相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 结构化领域约束、物理验证和仿真反馈能够将LLM从脆弱的网表生成器转变为实用的PIC设计代理，能够产生可制造的布局。PICasso展示了AI辅助PIC设计的可行路径，但功能规格满足度（52%）仍有较大提升空间，暗示领域知识注入和仿真反馈的深度可以进一步增强。

Abstract: We present PICasso, an AI-assisted framework for automated synthesis, verification, and optimization of photonic integrated circuits (PICs) from natural-language specifications. PICasso couples a structured NL -> YAML -> GDS generation pipeline with PDK aware knowledge injection, automated placement and routing, DRC/LVS validation, and SAX-based photonic simulation. To systematically evaluate AI-driven photonic design, we introduce PIC-Set, a benchmark of 36 parameterized PIC design tasks spanning core photonic primitives and multi-component circuits. Using PIC-Set, we benchmark several state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs) under a unified evaluation protocol, including new metrics such as structural and functional $Spec@k$, optimization efficiency, and robustness under perturbations. Across the benchmark, PICasso significantly improves end-to-end specification satisfaction compared to vanilla LLM generation. Structural $Spec@3$ reaches up to 92.7% and functional $Spec@3$ up to 52% on high-complexity circuits. In addition, PICasso consistently reduces circuit insertion loss, lowering the mean loss from 4.98 dB to 3.25 dB (1.74 dB improvement) through simulation-guided optimization. These results demonstrate that structured domain constraints, physical verification, and simulation feedback transform LLMs from brittle netlist generators into practical PIC design agents capable of producing manufacturable layouts with competitive runtimes relative to manual GUI-based workflows.

</details>


### [20] [CIFQA: A Deterministic Tool-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Financial Query Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26114)
*Kunjesh Parekh,Anil Kumar Tiwari,Divya Saxena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CIFQA，一个确定性工具驱动的多智能体LLM框架，用于解决计算密集型金融问答问题。CIFQA将语言理解与数值执行分离，通过专门的智能体负责查询解析、路由、参数提取、计算规划和响应生成，同时使用确定性的Python工具执行金融计算和规则应用。在定期存款查询基准上达到95.54%的计算密集查询准确率和90.87%的整体准确率，显著优于直接LLM基线，且17B开源模型在该框架下优于更大规模的frontier模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在处理自然语言任务时表现优异，但在求解多步金融计算时经常产生数值错误但看似合理的答案。计算密集型的金融问答需要对结构化利率、时间条件、数值公式和基于规则的约束进行精确推理，而现有LLM方法在这方面存在明显不足，因此需要一种能够将语言理解与精确数值执行分离的解决方案。

Method: CIFQA采用多智能体架构，将流程分解为五个专门化角色：查询解释（query interpretation）、路由（routing）、参数提取（parameter extraction）、计算规划（computation planning）和响应生成（response generation）。数值计算和规则应用交由确定性的Python工具执行，而非依赖LLM的数值推理能力，从而确保金融计算的精确性和规则执行的可靠性。

Result: CIFQA在计算密集型查询上达到95.54%的准确率，整体准确率为90.87%，大幅超越即使提供完整公式、利率表和基准指令的直接LLM基线。消融实验表明，确定性组件（如精确利率查找、期限计算、滚动年度调整和提前支取逻辑）是性能的关键贡献因素。此外，基于17B开源模型的CIFQA框架在相同金融信息条件下优于显著更大的frontier模型，表明架构设计比模型规模对数值可靠性更重要。

Conclusion: CIFQA通过将LLM的语言能力与确定性的计算工具相结合，为计算密集型金融问答提供了有效的解决方案。虽然当前在定期存款查询上进行了评估，但该框架具有通用性，可推广至其他计算密集型的金融推理任务，为金融领域的精确数值推理提供了一种架构层面的设计范式。

Abstract: Calculation-intensive financial question answering requires exact reasoning over structured rates, temporal conditions, numerical formulas, and rule-based constraints. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly on natural language tasks, they often produce numerically incorrect yet plausible answers when solving multi-step financial calculations. To address this limitation, we introduce CIFQA (Calculation-Intensive Financial Query Answering), a deterministic tool-grounded multi-agent LLM framework for financial question answering. CIFQA separates language understanding from numerical execution by assigning specialized agents to query interpretation, routing, parameter extraction, computation planning, and response generation, while deterministic Python-based tools perform financial calculations and rule application. We instantiate CIFQA for fixed deposit query answering and evaluate it on a curated benchmark of fixed deposit queries. CIFQA achieves 95.54% accuracy on calculation-intensive queries and 90.87% overall accuracy, substantially outperforming direct LLM baselines even when provided with complete formulas, rate cards, and benchmark instructions. Ablation studies show that deterministic components such as exact rate lookup, tenure computation, rolling-year adjustment, and premature-withdrawal logic are critical contributors to performance. Notably, a 17B open-source backbone operating within CIFQA outperforms substantially larger frontier models evaluated with the same financial information, demonstrating that architectural design is a more important determinant of numerical reliability than model scale. While evaluated on fixed deposit queries, CIFQA provides a generalizable framework for calculation-intensive financial reasoning tasks.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出CARL，一种基于自生成强化学习的闭环框架，用于通过最小局部扰动自主发现和控制Lenia连续元胞自动机中的自组织模式（如孤立子），并实现零样本泛化。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 引入基于autotelic强化学习的闭环框架：智能体自主采样多样化目标，学习目标条件化策略，通过最小局部扰动干预复杂系统。在Lenia连续元胞自动机上构建CARL系统，智能体通过少量局部扰动实现目标，并训练于多样目标、更新规则和随机初始状态下。

Result: CARL在多种Lenia更新规则下以高于启发式基线的方式发现稳定孤立子；学会通过少量干预控制已有孤立子的运动方向；人类可通过高层方向指令实时引导孤立子穿越迷宫环境；策略在分布外条件下实现零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了闭环自动学习方法在复杂系统探索和控制中的有效性，为构建能够自主或人类引导下发现和控制涌现现象的人工实验智能体开辟了道路。

Abstract: Existing methods for exploring cellular automata and other complex systems mostly operate in open loop: they set initial conditions, execute a full simulation, and observe the outcome, without intervening during execution. We introduce a closed-loop framework based on autotelic reinforcement learning, in which an agent autonomously samples diverse goals and learns a goal-conditioned policy to intervene in a complex system through minimal, local perturbations. We instantiate this framework on Lenia, a continuous cellular automaton known for life-like self-organizing patterns, in an agentic system we call CARL, and demonstrate three capabilities. First, CARL discovers stable solitons across a wide range of Lenia update rules at a higher rate than heuristic baselines. Second, it learns to steer the movement direction of existing solitons with few interventions, showing that CARL can control self-organizing patterns, not only create them. Third, humans can use trained agents to guide solitons through maze environments in real time by specifying high-level directional commands that the agent translates into low-level interventions. Trained across diverse goals, update rules, and random initial states, the agents acquire policies that generalize zero-shot to various out-of-distribution conditions. These results suggest a path toward artificial experimentalist agents that, autonomously or with human guidance, discover and control emergent phenomena in complex systems.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文发现了能源预测中的"精度-效率悖论"：高精度的复杂能源预测模型在边缘环境中可能反而导致净能量亏损，原因在于推理能耗和电池老化共同构成的能量损失超过了精度提升带来的节能收益。为此提出了一种总拥有成本（TCO）框架，将推理能耗与电池老化统一量化为能量损失，以最小化净能量损失为目标进行能源预测模型设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源预测追求高精度以提升能效、减少能源浪费，这一目标同样适用于军事系统等关键边缘环境中的设备端预测。然而，作者识别出一个关键悖论：高精度的复杂模型虽然预测更准、名义上能节省更多能源，但其在边缘设备上的推理能耗以及引发的电池老化，可能导致实际的净能量亏损，反而违背了节能初衷。

Method: 提出一个面向能源预测的总拥有成本（TCO）框架，核心创新在于将推理过程中的直接能量消耗与电池老化效应统一视为"能量损失"形式——因为电池老化意味着系统未来能量承载能力的物理耗散。该框架对两者进行一体化建模和量化，并以此作为选择或设计能源预测模型的优化目标。

Result: 实验表明，在热敏感的边缘环境中，复杂架构凭借更高精度所节省的能量，往往被其高计算强度所导致的总体能量损失（推理能耗+电池老化）所抵消甚至超过，从而验证了精度-效率悖论的存在以及TCO框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 高精度并不等于高能效，能源预测模型的选择不能仅以预测精度为依据。在边缘环境特别是热敏感场景中，必须将推理能耗和电池老化纳入统一的能量损失考量，采用TCO视角来指导模型架构设计，才能实现真正的净能量节约。

Abstract: Energy forecasting aims to maximize accuracy to ensure energy efficiency by reducing energy waste, an objective that applies equally to on-device forecasting for mission-critical edge environments, including military systems. However, this paper identifies the Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox: high-precision energy forecasting models can ironically trigger a net energy deficit. This stems from both edge AI's inference energy consumption and battery aging. We propose a Total Cost of Ownership (TCO) framework for energy forecasting, designed to minimize net energy loss. This framework treats not only inference energy consumption but also battery aging as a unified form of energy loss, as degradation represents a physical dissipation of the system's future energy-carrying capacity. We demonstrate that in thermally sensitive edge environments, energy saved by the superior precision of complex architectures is often outweighed by the total energy lost through their high operational intensity.

</details>

Result: AI生成的文献综述需要人工监督才能满足学术出版标准；长上下文窗口使LLM能够整合更广泛的信息并保持长输入的连贯性，但同时加剧了内容重复、关键工作遗漏以及偏重描述而非综合的问题。

Abstract: Our research focuses on evaluating literature reviews generated in short and long context settings of large language models (LLMs) to investigate the impact of context window on the quality of AI-generated literature reviews and the role of AI in supporting literature review writing. Twenty AI-generated literature reviews based on research sources from Semantic Scholar and Arxiv were evaluated by two researchers across 15 dimensions. Our findings reveal that AI-generated literature reviews require human oversight to meet academic publishing standards. As context windows increase, LLMs can incorporate broader information and maintain coherence across longer inputs, but they also exacerbate issues such as content repetition, omission of critical work, and a tendency towards descriptiveness over synthesis. Our work shows that AI-generated reviews can provide foundational overviews, but their output must be critically evaluated and refined by domain experts. Future research should consider integrating other LLMs and fine-tuned models in different domains with hybrid approaches that combine human expertise with AI capabilities to address the limitations identified in this study.

</details>


### [24] [Methodological and Conceptual Framework for 5D Multi-Table Analysis: A Unified Approach for Complex Data Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26149)
*Edouard Lansiaux,Hugo Kazzi,Aurélien Loison,Slim Hammadi,Emmanuel Chazard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出关系超图Transformer（RHT），一种用于多表学习的统一架构，将关系数据库表示为超图，学习五维嵌入（PentE），并采用稀疏关系注意力机制（复杂度与平均关系度成正比而非实体数量平方）。在Synthea合成EHR数据集上的多标签预测任务中，RHT在嵌入语义连贯性上优于表格、关系和图基线，同时保持计算可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗等领域的关系数据（多表数据）学习面临多重挑战，包括数据量大、高维变量、高基数分类特征、复杂的表间依赖以及重复的时间观测。现有方法难以同时处理这些复杂性，需要一种统一且可扩展的架构来有效建模关系数据库。

Method: RHT将关系数据库表示为超图，通过学习五维嵌入（PentE）来捕捉实体间的复杂关系，并设计稀疏关系注意力机制，其计算复杂度与平均关系度成正比（而非实体数量的平方）。该架构统一定义了超图构建、五维嵌入学习及稀疏注意力计算流程。

Result: 在Synthea合成EHR数据集的SNOMED CT条件码多标签预测任务中，RHT与表格、关系及时序图基线对比，产生了语义更连贯的嵌入且计算可扩展。XGBoost在罕见编码召回率上最高，而RHT在嵌入语义连贯性上最强。消融研究量化了各架构组件的贡献。MIMIC-IV上的临床验证计划在PhysioNet认证后开展。

Conclusion: RHT为多表关系数据学习提供了一个统一且有理论复杂度保障的解决方案，在嵌入质量和计算效率之间取得了良好平衡。尽管在罕见编码预测上不如XGBoost，但RHT在嵌入语义连贯性方面的优势表明其潜在的实际应用价值，后续将在真实临床数据上进一步验证。

Abstract: Multi-table learning remains a major challenge in machine learning for healthcare and other complex information systems. Relational data combine several sources of complexity, including large data volume, high-dimensional variables, high-cardinality categorical features, complex inter-table dependencies, and repeated temporal observations. We introduce the Relational Hypergraph Transformer (RHT), a unified architecture that represents relational databases as hypergraphs, learns pentadimensional embeddings (PentE), and performs sparse relational attention with complexity proportional to the average relational degree rather than the square of the number of entities. We formally define the architecture, derive the complexity of its attention mechanism, and provide an open-source reference implementation. We evaluate RHT on the public Synthea synthetic electronic health record dataset using multi-label prediction of SNOMED CT condition codes per encounter, a task characterized by high categorical cardinality and long-tailed label distributions. Comparisons with tabular, relational, and temporal graph baselines show that RHT produces more semantically coherent embeddings while remaining computationally scalable. In this benchmark, the highest rare-code recall is achieved by XGBoost, whereas RHT attains the strongest embedding semantic coherence. We also report ablation studies quantifying the contribution of each architectural component. Clinical validation on MIMIC-IV is planned following PhysioNet credentialing. Source code and experimental protocols are provided in the accompanying repository.

</details>


### [25] [Leveraging Large Language Models for Systematic Literature Review of Disease Spread Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26150)
*Orhan Yagizer Cinar,Timur Emre Ozkose,Emma Von Hoene,Amira Roess,Taylor Anderson,Hamdi Kavak*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的最新进展为自动化研究流程（包括系统文献综述）创造了新机会，本研究旨在探索LLM在系统文献综述自动化方面的潜力和局限。

Method: 研究开发了一个LLM流水线，用于从536篇经同行评审的基于代理建模论文中提取与模型相关的信息，并将结果与人工执行的系统文献综述进行比较。评估了GPT-4.1和GPT-5.0两种模型的准确性，并分析了LLM之间的一致性作为质量指标的可行性。

Result: GPT-4.1和GPT-5.0的论文级准确率分别约为77.95%和81.67%。字段级准确率范围为32.40%至100.00%，较为复杂或主观的字段可靠性较低。LLM间的一致性可作为输出质量的潜在指标：低一致性可能表明幻觉，高一致性与低准确率结合则可能指向人工数据集存在噪声或错误。

Conclusion: 该研究为提示工程（prompt development）提供了实用见解，并阐述了LLM在建模与仿真领域进行全规模系统文献综述的潜力与局限。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have created new opportunities to streamline and potentially automate many research processes, including systematic literature reviews (SLRs). This study reports an LLM pipeline development for extracting model-relevant information from 536 peer-reviewed agent-based modeling papers. We compare the results with those of a human-conducted SLR. Our results show paper-level accuracies of approximately 77.95% for GPT-4.1 and 81.67% for GPT-5.0. Field-level accuracy ranges from 32.40% to 100.00%, with more complex or subjective fields performing less reliably. Importantly, we find that agreement between LLMs is a potential indicator of output quality: low agreement may signal hallucinations, whereas high agreement combined with low accuracy may point to noise or errors in the human dataset. Overall, our study provides practical insights into prompt development and highlights both the potential and limitations of using LLMs for full-scale SLRs in the modeling and simulation domain.

</details>


### [26] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence for Customer Churn Prediction in Telecommunications: A Framework for CRM Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26151)
*Sandeep Gaddamwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对电信客户流失预测中"高性能模型不可解释"的问题，在IBM Telco基准数据集上对比了四种分类器（Logistic Regression、Random Forest、XGBoost、LightGBM），发现Logistic Regression AUC-ROC最强（0.8411），LightGBM准确率最高（78.42%），四者在0.011的AUC区间内基本持平。论文结合全球SHAP排序与实例级SHAP/LIME解释，提出四层CRM集成架构，将风险分数和归因向量转化为分层分群与保留行动模板，预计最高风险五分位客户定位可将整体流失率降低3.3-5.3个百分点，每个营销周期节省约$199K-$319K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信行业每月约1.9%的客户流失侵蚀数十亿美元收入，虽然预测模型能准确识别风险客户，但高性能的集成和非线性架构缺乏可解释性，保留专员仅凭概率分数无法设计个性化干预方案。本文旨在弥合预测模型与一线CRM工作流之间的可解释性鸿沟。

Method: 在IBM Telco基准（7,043条记录、19个特征、26.5%流失率，训练集上用SMOTE平衡至50%）上对比Logistic Regression、Random Forest、XGBoost、LightGBM四个分类器，采用5折交叉验证评估，并通过全局SHAP排序和实例级SHAP/LIME分解提供双重粒度解释，最后提出四层CRM集成架构（风险分数→分层分群→行动模板→重训反馈循环）。

Result: Logistic Regression取得最强AUC-ROC（0.8411），LightGBM最高准确率（78.42%），四模型AUC均落在0.831-0.841的0.011窄区间内，5折交叉验证显示领先模型实质持平。全局SHAP显示任期、总费用和月付合约是主导流失信号。聚焦最高风险五分位的策略预计可将整体流失降低3.3-5.3个百分点，每营销周期节约$199K-$319K。

Conclusion: 可解释性并非牺牲性能的代价——即使在可解释模型中，准确率也能达到与黑盒模型同等的水平。通过全局与实例级解释机制及四层CRM集成架构，模型可以从"仅输出概率分数"升级为"可操作的保留决策支持系统"，实现显著的经济收益。

Abstract: Subscriber attrition is a costly, persistent challenge for telecommunications providers, with monthly churn of roughly 1.9% in mature markets eroding billions in revenue annually. Predictive models can flag at-risk customers accurately, yet they are routinely excluded from frontline CRM workflows because high-performing ensemble and non-linear architectures are opaque: a retention specialist cannot design a personalised intervention from a probability score alone, without knowing why a subscriber is at risk. This paper addresses that gap. We benchmark four classifiers--Logistic Regression, Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM--on the IBM Telco Customer Churn benchmark (7,043 records; 19 features; 26.5% churn, balanced to 50% via SMOTE on the training partition only). Logistic Regression attains the strongest AUC-ROC (0.8411) and LightGBM the highest accuracy (78.42%); all four fall within a 0.011 AUC band (0.831--0.841), and 5-fold cross-validation confirms the leading models are effectively tied. Explanations are delivered at two granularities: a global SHAP ranking identifying tenure, total charges, and month-to-month contract as the dominant churn signals, and instance-level SHAP and LIME decompositions that expose the drivers behind each prediction. Building on these outputs, we introduce a four-layer CRM integration architecture that converts risk scores and attribution vectors into tiered segmentation, maps top features to structured retention-action templates, and routes campaign outcomes into a retraining feedback loop. Targeting the highest-risk quintile is projected to cut overall churn by 3.3--5.3 percentage points, preserving an estimated $199K--$319K per campaign cycle.

</details>


### [27] [EEG-to-Report: An Annotation and Feature-Text Framework for Training Language Models on Clinical EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26153)
*Xuan-The Tran,Le Trung Kien Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍EEG-to-Report，一个基于浏览器的注释与特征文本框架，将常规EEG阅片流程与AI数据集构建相结合，实现多模态EEG-语言模型监督数据的生成与自动报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床EEG报告仍以人工为主、耗时费力，且现有EEG软件生态系统无法产生训练现代语言模型所需的结构化EEG-文本监督数据。大多数工具仅聚焦可视化或预处理，缺乏支持生成高质量AI数据集的完整工作流。

Method: 提出EEG-to-Report框架，包含：(1)多格式EEG数据导入与通道标准化；(2)交互式查看器与多模态注释层（文本+语音转写）；(3)特征提取引擎计算谱、时域、熵、Hjorth、连接性和尖峰相关描述子；(4)便携JSON模式存储对齐的特征-文本对；(5)自动报告模块，集成卷积网络集成与大型语言模型生成临床叙述草稿供神经科医生审阅。

Result: 通过试点注释证明EEG-to-Report能够简化注释工作流并生成可编辑的报告草稿，产生对齐的特征-文本对以训练多模态EEG-语言模型。

Conclusion: EEG-to-Report为自动化EEG报告系统提供了可复用的基础，弥合了临床EEG审阅与AI数据集构建之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Clinical electroencephalography (EEG) reporting remains largely manual and time-consuming, and current EEG software ecosystems do not produce the structured EEG-text supervision needed for training modern language models. Most toolboxes focus on visualization or preprocessing, providing limited support for workflows that generate high-quality datasets for AI. We introduce EEG-to-Report, a browser-based annotation and feature-text framework that links routine EEG review with the construction of AI-ready datasets. The framework integrates multi-format EEG ingestion, channel standardization, and an interactive viewer with a multimodal annotation layer that combines typed text and transcribed voice notes. For each annotated segment, a feature extraction engine computes a standardized set of spectral, temporal, entropy, Hjorth, connectivity, and spike-related descriptors, stored alongside clinical descriptions in a portable JSON schema. This yields aligned feature-text pairs designed to supervise multimodal EEG-language models. The framework also includes an auto-report module that couples an ensemble of convolutional networks with a large language model to draft clinical narratives for neurologist review. Using pilot annotations, we describe how EEG-to-Report streamlines annotation workflows and produces editable draft reports, providing a reusable foundation for automated EEG reporting systems.

</details>


### [28] [Selection Bias Correction in Retail Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26156)
*Spandan Ghose Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该模拟研究通过400次蒙特卡洛重复实验、覆盖四种数据生成场景，比较了逆概率加权（IPW）与分层法在零售长尾分布中的通胀估计修正效果，发现分层法在大多数场景下表现更优（尤其在违背正性假设时可达116倍优势），而IPW仅在平滑多项式关系下略占优势，表明分层法是零售长尾场景下更安全的工程选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售情报通常聚焦于高销量热门产品，忽略利基商品组成的"长尾"，可能导致经济指标（如通胀指数）存在选择性偏差。现有修正方法（如IPW）在长尾场景下的表现缺乏系统性验证，因此需要比较不同修正方法在多样数据生成过程中的鲁棒性，以期为实践提供可靠的工程指导。

Method: 通过400次蒙特卡洛模拟重复实验，在四种数据生成场景（对齐阶跃函数、平滑梯度、错位断裂、多项式关系）下，对五种规格的逆概率加权（IPW）方法以及不同分层数的分层法进行比较测试，评估其在通胀估计中的修正效果。

Result: 分层法在四种场景中的三种表现更优，即使在边界与总体断裂故意错位时仍能保持中位误差低于0.04个百分点（相对IPW有116倍优势）；IPW仅在平滑多项式关系下以中位误差0.007pp优于分层法的0.013pp。在阶跃函数场景中，即便是具备完美结构知识的Oracle IPW也达到了6.06pp的误差，远劣于分层法的0.008pp。

Conclusion: IPW在零售长尾场景中表现不佳的根本原因是正性假设（Positivity Assumption）的严重违背（选择概率差异可达90% vs. 1%），而非方法论本身的缺陷。在存在严重正性违背的零售长尾分布中，分层法是更稳健、更安全的工程选择。

Abstract: Retail intelligence often relies on monitoring popular, high-velocity products, potentially biasing economic indicators by ignoring the "long tail" of niche items. This simulation study investigates selection bias in inflation estimation and compares correction methods across diverse data-generating processes. Through 400 Monte Carlo replications spanning four scenarios--aligned step functions, smooth gradients, misaligned breaks, and polynomial relationships--we test the robustness of Inverse Probability Weighting (IPW) with five specifications against stratification with varying strata counts. Our findings reveal fundamental limits of weighting methods in retail long-tail contexts: stratification achieves superior performance in three of four scenarios, maintaining sub-0.04pp median error even when boundaries deliberately misalign with population breaks (116x advantage over IPW). However, IPW with spline propensity models wins under smooth polynomial relationships (median error 0.007pp vs. 0.013pp), demonstrating context-dependency. Critically, even an oracle IPW specification with perfect structural knowledge achieves 6.06pp error compared to stratification's 0.008pp in step-function scenarios. This reflects violation of the Positivity Assumption--a fundamental causal inference requirement--rather than IPW methodological inferiority. When selection probabilities differ dramatically (90% vs. 1%), weighting methods operate outside their theoretical design envelope. These results demonstrate that stratification provides a safer engineering choice in retail long-tail distributions with severe positivity violations.

</details>


### [29] [GROUND: Reducing Hallucinations in LLM-Based Enterprise Analytics Through Governed Semantic Definitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26157)
*Aravind Sasidharan Pillai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GROUND框架，通过将LLM生成的分析约束在受治理的语义层上（提供批准的指标定义、绑定用户意图、并在执行前验证生成的SQL），解决企业数据仓库自然语言分析中的幻觉指标、非法连接、错误粒度、不安全数据访问等问题。在100题合成基准和真实NHTSA数据上的实验表明，GROUND是唯一在所有六个评估类别中零幻觉的系统，其强制保证（尤其是过滤器和行级安全）在所有模型上均保持零违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据仓库的自然语言分析在生产环境中面临严重限制，包括幻觉指标、非法连接、错误粒度、不安全数据访问和不支持的解释。现有text-to-SQL系统通常基于数据库模式或检索文档来引导生成，但企业报告还需要受治理的业务语义——批准的指标、维度、连接路径、过滤器和行级安全。缺乏对这些治理约束的强制保证，导致生产级应用无法信任LLM生成的分析结果。

Method: GROUND（Governed Retrieval Over Unified Normalized Definitions）框架的核心方法包括：(1) 提供一个受治理的语义层，包含批准的定义；(2) 将用户意图绑定到受治理的指标和维度；(3) 在执行前，根据模式、指标、连接、粒度、过滤器、安全性和成本规则验证生成的SQL；(4) 存在违规时进行重试或拒绝（abstain）。实验设计为100题合成企业报告基准，比较了纯模式text-to-SQL、模式RAG、纯语义引导三种基线，并在真实NHTSA车辆安全数据上用于工编写的黄金答案进行验证，同时在不同提供商的四种模型上进行对抗性测试。

Result: GROUND是唯一在所有六个评估类别中零测量幻觉的系统，而未受治理的系统在许多问题上违反行级安全。仅语义引导（有精确指标定义但无访问策略）仍会泄露数据，证明治理不能仅靠指标保真度替代。真实NHTSA数据上结果可复现，且在所有四种模型上，GROUND的强制保证（尤其是过滤器和行级安全）保持零违规，而依赖判断的行为（如拒绝未定义指标）仍可能出错。

Conclusion: GROUND证明了通过治理语义层强制执行约束是确保企业LLM数据分析安全可靠的关键，单纯提供准确语义定义而缺乏策略强制不足以防止数据泄露。框架的强制保证具有跨模型泛化能力，但判断依赖型行为仍有改进空间。治理必须作为独立于指标保真度的强制机制而被架构性地嵌入系统。

Abstract: Natural-language analytics over enterprise data warehouses is increasingly important, but production use is limited by hallucinated metrics, invalid joins, wrong grain, unsafe data access, and unsupported explanations. Existing text-to-SQL systems often ground generation in database schemas or retrieved documentation, while enterprise reporting also requires governed business semantics: approved metrics, dimensions, join paths, filters, and row-level security. This paper introduces GROUND, Governed Retrieval Over Unified Normalized Definitions, a framework that constrains LLM-generated analytics to a governed semantic layer. GROUND supplies approved definitions, binds user intent to governed metrics and dimensions, and validates generated SQL against schema, metric, join, grain, filter, security, and cost rules before execution. On violations, it retries or abstains.
  In a 100-question synthetic enterprise-reporting benchmark, GROUND is compared with direct schema-only text-to-SQL, schema-RAG, and semantic-only grounding under one shared model. GROUND is the only system free of measured hallucinations across all six evaluated categories, while ungoverned systems violate row-level security on many questions. A semantic-only condition with exact metric definitions but no access policy still leaks data, showing that governance cannot be replaced by metric fidelity alone. The findings are replicated on real U.S. NHTSA vehicle-safety data with independent hand-authored gold and tested on an adversarial set across four models from three providers. GROUND's enforced guarantees, especially filters and row-level security, hold with zero violations on every model, while judgment-dependent behaviors such as refusing undefined metrics remain fallible.

</details>

*Viorica Rozina Chifu,Tudor Cioara,Vasile Ofrim,Liana Toderean,Ionut Anghel,Laura Daniele,Cornelis Bouter*
